### 2026-06-09 — first live run of P1: BLOCKING join bug + H1-substrate reality
- Pushed the session's work (origin/main synced) and ran the engine against the live DB. **Invocation gap:** bare `swing.exe` fails `ModuleNotFoundError: research` (research/ isn't an installed package + repo root not on sys.path); ran with `PYTHONPATH=<repo root>`. Affects ALL research diagnostics (pre-existing pattern), worth a fix.
- **Result: 42 unique signals, 100% → `no_canonical_detection`, ZERO priced trades, empty scorecard.** The funnel honesty surfaced it cleanly (no fabricated result).
- **Root cause (blocking design bug):** the canonical-detection rule `detection.pivot == candidate.pivot` is a FALSE PREMISE. `detection.pivot` (structural_anchors.evidence.pivot_price) is the PATTERN-geometric pivot — per pattern class, often `0.0` (double_bottom_w / cup_with_handle); `candidate.pivot` is the SCREENING pivot. Different quantities (WULF 25.86/0.0 vs 27.47), never coincide. Synthetic fixtures forced them equal → passed 6+3 Codex rounds; only the live run falsified it (the **synthetic-vs-real-emitter drift** gotcha — already in CLAUDE.md). **Fix:** re-adopt the R3 direction — recompute entry from `candidate.pivot` over the shared frozen forward bars (all (run,ticker) detections share the OHLC; no look-ahead since forward obs are post-detection). A focused correction arc.
- **Two substrate realities the run exposed:** (1) all 42 signals are `watch` — ZERO aplus — so even fixed, the engine prices H2/H3/H4, NOT H1; **H1 stays gated on rare aplus fires.** (2) The log is tiny/young (4 runs, 2026-06-05→09, 219 forward obs) → ~5-session walks → nearly everything open-at-horizon; needs months. **Refined thesis:** P1 accelerates the WATCH hypotheses fast; H1 accrues only as aplus fires (still faster than hand-trading, but NOT signal-pace as I'd framed it). Correcting my own earlier overstatement.

### 2026-06-09 — entry/join correction spec: Codex-converged + QA PASS
- Dispatched the correction brainstorm to a fresh instance (brief `c84369cc`); it returned a Codex-converged spec (`6fd664f7`; 3 rounds, 7 major resolved, `NO_NEW_CRITICAL_MAJOR`). **QA PASS** — independently re-verified the load-bearing claim against the live DB: no-look-ahead holds (**0/219** obs violate `observation_date > data_asof_date`; first obs is ON `detection_date`, which correctly corrects my brief's wrong "strictly after detection_date" framing). Trailers clean.
- **Design (surgical, sound):** entry recomputed as first forward bar `high >= candidate.pivot`; canonical detection demoted to bar-source-only (longest chain, prefix-invariant-checked); removed the falsified `detection.pivot==candidate.pivot` match + `no_canonical_detection` + a SECOND latent bug (`inconsistent_trigger_state` — per-pattern pivots legitimately differ, 8/42 groups); new `no_candidate_pivot` reason. Honest V1 limitation: no close-confirmation admits intraday-touch entries → realistic arm slightly optimistic, mitigated by a reported `entry_bar_weak_close` diagnostic. Invocation-gap fix folded in → ALL research diagnostics now turnkey from the installed entry point. Spec: `2026-06-09-shadow-expectancy-entry-join-correction-design.md`.
- **Substrate reality (restated honestly in the spec):** even fixed, the current log prices ≈zero — every `candidate.pivot` sits above its forward highs; nothing has broken out in the thin 2–3 session windows. The fix unblocks the MECHANISM; priced samples accrue as the log matures + breakouts occur. Success criterion correctly reset to *funnel-routes-correctly + a breakout golden-fixture prices end-to-end*, NOT immediate H1 numbers. **Next: writing-plans.**

### 2026-06-09 — correction SHIPPED + the attribution-coverage finding
- Executing-plans returned (brief `5a5e3b8c`), Codex-converged round 2, **QA PASS + MERGED `31e7441c`** (L2 held — only `swing/cli.py`; clean 3-way merge onto Phase-16-Arc-2 main; zero co-author trailers; **7516 fast tests green** on the merged head). Codex caught a real honesty bug (attributed zero-observation signals mis-routed to `never_triggered` → `missing_observations`). Worktree removed. Re-ran the engine **turnkey** — the invocation fix is confirmed (runs from the installed entry point without `PYTHONPATH`).
- **The fix WORKED + surfaced the next binding constraint.** Live funnel: 210 detections → 42 unique signals, **`no_canonical_detection` wall GONE**, all 42 join+collapse+reach attribution — but **100% `matched_no_hypothesis`**. Bottleneck moved from the collapse bug (fixed) to **attribution coverage**: all 42 are `watch` (zero aplus); the active hypotheses are NARROW cohorts (H1 aplus / H2 watch∩proximity_20ma-only / H4 watch∩risk_feasibility-only) that don't cover the BROAD #23-widened watch pool. Honest output, not a defect.
- **Three independent reasons the engine prices ≈zero today** (all substrate/coverage, none a defect): (1) attribution coverage gap (above); (2) no breakouts — every `candidate.pivot` sits above its forward highs in the thin 2–3 session windows; (3) no aplus → H1 starved.
- **My recommendation (research-director call):** add a **broad-watch-baseline hypothesis** (bucket==watch, any miss-set) so the engine prices the population the log ACTUALLY contains — which is also the operator's actual trading practice (12/16 live trades were watch). Highest-value next step (the narrow cohorts almost never occur); answers "does the widened watch pool have mechanical edge?" without risking capital. Routes through V2.1 §VII governance (a registry amendment / new migration). Caveat: necessary-not-sufficient — even with coverage, prices ≈zero until the log matures (breakouts). **The instrument is DONE; the measurement now awaits a registry decision + market time, not more engineering.**

### 2026-06-09 — Session 2 (fresh instance): operator GO — both arcs commissioned
- Operator approved P1-NOW and added a second commission: the engine must run **automatically in the operational drumbeat**. - **Pre-dispatch grounding surfaced two load-bearing design hazards** (verified in code, not carried from the handoff): (1) the engine's attribution is a thin wrapper over the PRODUCTION matcher (`research/harness/shadow_expectancy/attribution.py` → `swing/recommendations/hypothesis.py:match_candidate_to_hypotheses`), and the engine routes **>1 match → `multi_match` → unattributed** (`run.py` ~L121) — a naive `bucket==watch` rule would cannibalize H2/H4 attribution the moment their signals appear → the brief mandates **fallback semantics** (baseline = watch not otherwise hypothesized); (2) the same matcher feeds the LIVE recommendation surfaces (`hypothesis_prefill.py`, `dashboard.py` ×2) — an always-matching hypothesis would flood them and outrank H2/H4 via distance-to-target → the brief proposes opt-in containment (`include_baseline=False` default; shadow engine passes True). Also verified live: registry v25 has H1/H2/H4 active, H3 `closed-target-met`; matcher's documented fifth-hypothesis extension point at `hypothesis.py` ~L124; latest export (`20260610T063533Z`) re-confirms 42/42 `matched_no_hypothesis`.
- **Briefs authored + committed:** `docs/broad-watch-baseline-hypothesis-brainstorming-dispatch-brief.md` (migration 0026 v25→v26; proposed pre-registration: name 'Broad-watch baseline', target 30 shadow-priced, baseline criteria with both outcomes pre-committed; tripwires 5/5.0 schema-mandated) and `docs/phase16-shadow-expectancy-drumbeat-integration-commissioning-brief.md` (Phase 16 **Arc 5**). **Placement recommendation:** new best-effort step `shadow_expectancy` AFTER `_step_export`, BEFORE `complete` (data final after `pattern_observe`; last slot protects operator-facing outputs; lease serialization + Arc-1 step-timing/pipeline.log observability for free); **subprocess** of the installed CLI, not an in-process `research/` import (bifurcation boundary + crash isolation); rejected: pre-run (one session stale) and external scheduler (no lease coordination, drift). Arcs are independent — land in either order.
- Next: operator dispatches both inline prompts; research director QAs the brainstorm returns.

### 2026-06-10 — P0 commissioned; the labeling question answered
- **Operator commissioned P0** (tuition-vs-error instrumentation). Brief authored + committed: `docs/p0-tuition-vs-error-instrumentation-brainstorming-dispatch-brief.md`. Core design direction: a trade-level `entry_intent` nullable enum via migration 0027 (v27), set-at-entry/correctable-at-review, operator-driven backfill of the 16 historical trades, surfaces FACETED not regraded (the semantic contract: `process_grade` measures execution-given-intent, so the historical "A on designed losers" grades are correct — the fix is display conflation). Orthogonality lock: a designed trade with a slip (VIR `STOP_NOT_PLACED`) still counts as a violation. Measurement chain untouched. The Phase 16 orchestrator is running the run-#98 perf arc in parallel — not my lane.
- **Labeling answer (operator Q):** yes, `Broad-watch baseline` on live watch trades is MANUAL — the entry-form prefill (`lookup_active_recommendation_label` → matcher default `include_baseline=False`) will never suggest it, by the containment design (the frozen 0026 notes: "live recommendation surfaces never surface it"). Operational rule given: take the narrow prefill when one fires (H1/H2/H4 — narrower attribution wins, matching the fallback complement), else hand-type `Broad-watch baseline` (exact name, then optional ` ...`/`; ...` suffix per the 3-rule prefix contract). If manual proves annoying, opting the ENTRY-FORM PREFILL ONLY into the baseline is a one-line + tests mini-amendment — but it touches the frozen notes' "never surface it" language, so it routes as a deliberate governance edit; recommended living with manual at the current ~trade-per-week rate.
- **CORRECTION (2026-06-10, web-GUI mechanics):** my "hand-type the label" guidance was WRONG for the web — the entry form's `hypothesis_label` is a display-only span + HIDDEN input, server-resolved at form-render via `lookup_active_recommendation_label` (`trade_entry_form.html.j2:147-148`, `view_models/trades.py:542`; the V1 server-stamp discipline). No free-text hypothesis field and no post-hoc label editor exist in the web. With the prefill contained (`include_baseline=False`), web-entered watch trades persist `hypothesis_label=NULL`. The ONLY manual labeling path is the CLI `swing trade entry --hypothesis "Broad-watch baseline"`. Identification tell in the GUI: a watch name absent from the hyp-recs panel / entry form showing "Hypothesis: (none)". Consequently the prefill mini-amendment (opt `lookup_active_recommendation_label` ONLY into the baseline; fallback gate gives narrow-first for free) is now RECOMMENDED rather than optional — it is the only way the operator's web-first workflow yields labeled broad-watch trades; routes as a documented micro-arc separate from P0 (it amends the 0026 spec's prefill-containment letter; argument: the entry-form prefill is an ATTRIBUTION surface, not a recommendation surface). Awaiting operator go.
- **Labeling-effectiveness requirements elicited + Phase 16 arc commissioned (2026-06-10):** operator proposed pairing the missing watchlist-tagging feature with the labeling fix. Four operator decisions captured via structured Q&A: (1) tag shape = **pin flag + note** (not free-form tags, not an enum); (2) label source = **matcher-driven** (the prefill amendment; tags do NOT drive labels — labels always mirror shadow attribution); (3) pin semantics = **blocks removal only** (streaks keep counting; unpin → accumulated state applies); (4) the prefill amendment **folds into the Phase 16 arc** (not a separate micro-arc), with the research-director governance authorization granted in the brief (the 0026 spec §5.1 letter is amended by a dated addendum re-classifying the single-ticker entry-form/CLI prefill as an ATTRIBUTION surface; dashboard recommendation surfaces stay contained, regression-tested). Brief: `docs/phase16-watchlist-pin-and-labeling-effectiveness-commissioning-brief.md` (binding R1–R6; ground truth verified in code: no tag columns exist on `watchlist`; the absent-from-candidates skip at `service.py:70-71` flagged for the stale-pinned-row design). Coordination noted: P0 (in flight) and this arc both touch the entry form; migration numbers taken at branch time.

- **THE DEFECT (writer-side, sample-attrition not corruption):** `build_ohlc_today_json` (`swing/pipeline/temporal_metadata.py:149`) — the temporal-log construction barrier — guards completed-session + key-presence + provider but **NOT value finiteness**. A completed PAST session whose yfinance adjusted `Close` returned `NaN` (Arc-8 trailing-ragged artifact; O/H/L/V present, Close=NaN) was serialized into the immutable log. **103 forward obs / 19 tickers, ALL the 2026-06-10 session, all Close=NaN** (bounded — full 1287-row scan found non-finite OHLC ONLY on 06-10). Arc-8's `_trim_trailing_ragged` lives ONLY in `swing/data/ohlcv_archive.py` — never mirrored to this 2nd write path (two-path divergence, the recurring parallel-archive gotcha family).
- **Consequence + the engine's saving grace:** the engine's `validate_bars` (`run.py:147`) honestly rejects any chain containing a non-finite bar → `excluded/invalid_ohlc` BEFORE the simulator → **no R is ever poisoned** (today's 4 priced names hand-verified clean; the engine is robust — the defect is purely collection/storage). But the cost is throughput: **23 of 77 attributed signals (30%) permanently excluded** (manifest: `invalid_ohlc` 06-10:0 → 06-11:22 → stable 23) in a sample-starved measurement. **Observability gap:** `invalid_ohlc` is manifest-only — absent from `summary.md` and `weekly_glance.py` — so operator surfaces read all-clear (trigger 10/33, unattributed=0) while 30% silently dropped out. This is exactly the blind spot that hid the defect 06-10→06-13.
- **Posture deviation justified (watch-standard §6.4):** a correctness defect in the instrument that attrits ~30%, recurs, and is operator-invisible warrants fixing now (operator concurred, flagged HIGH).
- **Two RD→CHARC commissioning briefs authored + committed `c9205d1d` (pushed) + posted to CHARC** (`query`, thread `phase18-data-collection-integrity`, `20260613T205703Z`): (1) `docs/temporal-log-nan-writer-fix-commissioning-brief.md` — finiteness guard at the write barrier (mirror Arc-8 via a shared helper), LOCK validate_bars unchanged, open Qs = skip-vs-reject / the 103 existing rows (leave-vs-governed-backfill, cost it) / surface excluded reasons; (2) `docs/data-collection-health-monitor-commissioning-brief.md` — mechanize this audit as a read-only integrity monitor (the layer weekly_glance omits), key open Q = nightly-flag vs spin-up-only cadence, LOCKS read-only/don't-fork-funnel/ASCII/no-dep, RD owns the post-build watch-standard amendment. ### 2026-06-14 — Phase 18 opened; Arc 18-A (NaN writer fix) writing-plans QA'd; RD decision-#3 ruling (RD is merge-blocking)
- **Phase 17 CLOSED (orchestrator + CHARC clean-to-close);** the measurement chain held (17-A verified `evaluate_batch`/timings untouched). **Phase 18 (data-collection integrity) opened** — Arc 18-A = my temporal-log NaN-writer-fix commission. Writing-plans returned Codex-converged (plan `b82c5abb`, worktree `.worktrees/phase18-arc-a`), orchestrator-QA PASS. **Three-eyes merge gate: orchestrator QA + CHARC (C1–C3) + RD (measurement-integrity) — RD is MERGE-BLOCKING.**
- **Design (sound):** TWO layers — caller `_step_pattern_observe` SKIPs the non-finite bar with a `warnings_json` `reason="non_finite_ohlc"` (the primary, graceful path, mirroring the shipped `bar is None` precedent) + serializer `build_ohlc_today_json` ALSO raises (belt, unreachable in prod). Shared predicate `is_finite_ohlc` in new `swing/data/ohlcv_finiteness.py` (`math.isfinite`, OHLC-only, volume-exempt) — matches `validate_bars` exactly → **writer↔engine finiteness PARITY** (the property I most require; prevents a new two-path divergence).
- **Plan caught more than my brief (QA the divergence, directive #10):** a NaN-close bar ALSO drives a phantom `('triggered_open','entry_fired')` status transition at `_advance_status` (`NaN < x` False → `high ≥ pivot`) — corrupting the forward-obs STATUS field, not just the OHLC json. My audit missed this; the caller-skip prevents BOTH. Stronger than the commission.
- **RD decision-#3 ruling = KEEP the serializer raise** (posted charc,operator `20260614T023147Z`, thread `phase18-arc-a`). Reasoning: unreachable in prod (caller pre-skips) → ZERO effect on recorded data → can't threaten integrity by construction; fires ONLY on a future bypass, where fail-LOUD is the correct backstop for an immutable substrate (silent-skip would hide the bug). No lock conflict — my §4.1 skip-not-raise governs the CALLER (satisfied); LOCK 2 "add finiteness" satisfied by the raise. Concur w/ orchestrator lean; fallback (delete the raise) is acceptable but inferior (single-point protection, no fail-loud backstop).

- **2026-07-27 (cont.) — 21-A PLAN-STAGE GATE: PASS, five rulings; the plan caught me contradicting MYSELF and refused to resolve it (correct).** **G.1 — the conflict was real:** my 07-23 rd-state line ('a re-fire would open a NEW latch at the new pivot') vs today's ruling ('re-fire while armed = re-confirmation'). **Resolution: the plan's zone-escape DIAGNOSIS is right, but the fix is NOT a fourth clear condition.** Both my statements were partly right: 07-23 correctly saw FTRE's mandate had become UNACTIONABLE but mis-attributed the mechanism to re-firing; today correctly handled identical-value re-fires but over-generalized to all of them. **THE RULE: re-fire while armed → SAME pivot = re-confirmation (no fork, no re-freeze; SLDB 12); DIFFERENT pivot = the old latch CLEARS as SUPERSEDED and a new latch arms at the new frozen values.** The hazard in constraint 1 was never re-freezing per se — it was re-freezing SILENTLY under a resting order; a supersede is visible, and the panel must loudly show a stale resting order no longer matches. **No fourth clear condition: zone escape is a STATE ATTRIBUTE** ('ARMED — OUT OF ZONE'), because the resting order stays valid and fills on a pullback (clearing would desync from a live order — what tier-1 exists to prevent), and 'price won't return' is unknowable. **G.2 — MY ERROR, and a CIRCULAR one:** I invented '~20 sessions', recorded FTRE's horizon 08-17 from it, and the plan back-solved 20 from my own date citing my doc — my unverified assertion became its own evidence. explaining it surfaced that **H1's frozen win-rate leg requires 11 wins of 20 — a 55% raw win rate — which NO cohort this program has measured approaches** (broad-watch 22.1% @ n=312; A+ shadow closed 0/2; live 2/2 @ n=2, meaningless). **As written the criterion would likely REJECT a genuinely profitable trend system: its failure mode is a FALSE NEGATIVE ON SUCCESS.** The operator then argued mean-R is the primary edge metric and win rate secondary — **correct, and his strongest point was one he understated: win rate is MANIPULABLE BY EXIT POLICY (move the target nearer / stop further and it rises while expectancy falls), so it can move OPPOSITE to edge; mean R prices both legs and cannot be gamed that way.** I steelmanned the floor's real job (outlier-robustness on the mean-R claim at small n — live for us given the VSTS +27R artifact history) and reframed: **they are not competing measures — mean R measures edge, the floor GUARDS the mean-R claim.** He leaned pre-registered note; **I argued against his lean and he changed course**: the decisive argument is that **the split (mean-R pass / win-rate fail) is the MODAL SUCCESS CASE, not a contingency** — a note is right for an edge case, an amendment for the expected path. **I THEN STRESS-TESTED MY OWN PROPOSED GUARD AND REJECTED IT:** leave-one-out FAILS a genuinely profitable fat-tailed trend shape (30% win, +0.45 mean, one 10R winner → LOO −0.05) — reproducing the exact fat-tail penalty that disqualifies the Wilson floor. **Adopted instead: no single trade may contribute ≥50% of gross profit**, which passes every test case correctly. **AMENDED TEXT (operator-approved verbatim): mean R > 0 across the 20 closed labeled trades AND no single trade ≥50% of gross profit; no-winners → fails; win rate + Wilson LB REPORTED as diagnostics alongside median R and top-3 concentration, not gates.** **NON-NEGOTIABLE attached: the ORIGINAL criterion must remain readable in the record** (overwriting in place destroys the evidence of what was pre-registered — the whole point); mechanism is CHARC's, preservation is not. Its OWN migration (not bundled — a governance amendment must be independently auditable), next free number (the 0032-collision lesson). **TIMING is the load-bearing argument: H1 has 2 closed standard-epoch trades and no signal, so the motivation is the criterion's ARITHMETIC, not its outcome — unimpeachable now, indefensible in six months. The window is open precisely because nobody knows the answer.** Stated in the brief deliberately: **this does NOT make H1 statistically conclusive — at n=20 with fat tails no criterion can** (the Wilson floor couldn't either, it just failed differently); levers flagged-not-bundled (larger n, or shadow-cohort corroboration). **One open question left UNRESOLVED on purpose:** the criterion says '20 closed labeled trades' without pinning the cohort; the epoch contract settles it (standard-only; the pre-epoch `by_design` A+ trade must not count) but the text is silent — **flagged, NOT folded in, because he signed a specific text and adding downstream is what preserve-the-quote forbids.**
- **2026-07-29 (cont.) — COHORT clause ADDED to the H1 amendment on the operator's instruction; routed as a supersedes-flagged addendum (`20260729T071538Z`), brief updated in place (`aa122dd1`/`8246d386`).** The 20 are now pinned to **STANDARD-intent trades only** per the training-epoch declaration; pre-epoch `hypothesis_test_by_design` trades are settled tuition and don't count. **VERIFIED LIVE BEFORE WRITING IT IN — it changes a real number: three trades carry the `A+ baseline (aplus)` label — YOU (2026-05-04, `by_design`, PRE-EPOCH → excluded), VSTS (06-25, standard), AMN (07-01, standard). Without the clause a future reader could reasonably count 3/20; with it H1 stands at 2/20.** So the clause prevents settled tuition from being silently re-read as practice — exactly what the epoch declaration exists to forbid. It CITES the epoch contract, does not modify it; scope otherwise unchanged. **Process note worth keeping: this arrived as an ADDENDUM rather than in the original because I flagged the ambiguity and deliberately did NOT fold it in — the operator had signed a specific text, and adding to a signed text downstream is what preserve-the-quote forbids. He read the flag and ruled. That is the convention working as designed, not an oversight: the flag existed so he could make the call, and he made it.** Also fixed a brief internal inconsistency (§4 pointed at an open question §6 had since resolved).
- **2026-07-29 (cont.) — 21-B bucket questions RULED (tasks 5+9 unblocked).** Root cause first: `r_bucket_for`'s trailing `return decision_r` assigned unlisted dispositions **BY DEFAULT to the bucket that scores AGAINST the operator, silently** — two arrived that way and nobody ruled them. **RAISE-on-unlisted + a test asserting every disposition appears exactly once is worth more than either ruling, because the next unlisted disposition is one neither of us has thought of.** **Q1 `pending_live`: EXCLUDED from EVERY rate** (decision, away, discipline) and reported as its own visible category — an unterminated latch is NOT YET AN OBSERVATION; scoring it counts an unfinished observation as finished AND produces a number that moves underneath a monthly read without anyone editing it (the exact instability that retired FTRE as the acceptance case). Rates compute over TERMINAL, CLASSIFIABLE observations only; the ledger self-completes when a latch terminates. **Q2 `attested_was_away`: NEITHER bucket — it gets a THIRD.** Scoring as `decision_r` is clearly wrong (the away bucket exists to separate exactly this); but merging into `away_r` **reintroduces through the ATTESTATION door the flattering path I closed at the DEFAULT door** — a self-report about one's own diligence is biased toward the comfortable explanation, and 'I was away' is more comfortable than 'I saw it and didn't decide'. That would let **the away rate — the number that would justify automating his entries — be inflated by self-report**, the one number I least want softenable. **RULED: own category, excluded from the discipline signal (attestation honoured), and the away rate reported in TWO forms — OBJECTIVE (telemetry-only, GATES the stage-3 decision) and ATTESTED (+self-report, an explicit UPPER BOUND).** Bias direction is safe the other way (a self-flagellating attestation is conservative). **GENERAL PRINCIPLE NAMED (third application): never merge categories differing in EVIDENCE KIND even when outcomes agree.** Endorsed CHARC's lane: the coherence-pass finding (**a review-loop fix must REPLACE superseded text, not ANNOTATE it — annotation leaves the old rule in NORMATIVE voice, the next round reads it as live and files a finding, which is then also fixed by annotation: THE LOOP MANUFACTURES ITS OWN FINDINGS**) is a genuine gotcha, proven by round 21's CRITICAL surviving round 20's fix *because* round 20 annotated. And the no-scope-split call is right: 18 contradictions that were all PRESENTATION with the right answer already stated somewhere = a coherent design inside an unreadable document.


### 2026-07-29 — TENURE CLOSE: the generational handoff (this RD generation, Phases 19–21-partial)

Handing off at context capacity, mid-Phase-21, with nothing owed and nothing load-bearing living only in a mailbox — swept for it. Current state is NOT here: it is in [`docs/rd-state.md`](rd-state.md), overwritten as the fresh generation's boot surface at `edba7de6`. This entry is history only.

**What this tenure actually settled, in the order it will matter to a successor:**

**1. The A+ LATCH POSTURE — the largest doctrinal change, and it came from a live trade going wrong quietly.** The operator was on vacation when FTRE fired A+ on 07-20; his buy-stop-limit never triggered, the bucket flickered, and the setup went on to run +1.66R with no position. The diagnosis was not a bug — it was that the framework had no concept of a *standing mandate*. An A+ fire now LATCHES a GTC entry mandate at the fire's pivot plus a +3% cap, clearing only on fill / invalidation / horizon. Bucket flicker never clears it. Everything downstream — 21-A, 21-B, 21-F, 21-G, the whole Phase-21 shape — descends from that one adopted sentence, and it originated as an operator observation about his own missed trade, not as an engineering finding.

**2. FREEZE AT FIRE TIME, and I violated it within a day of issuing it.** I quoted FTRE's invalidation as 16.51 — the *current* value — when the fire-time value was 14.88, an error that would have cancelled a live mandate about 11% early. CHARC caught it by reading the row on disk. The rule stands (read the FIRE's `candidates` row, never the latest; pivots and stops drift while a latch is armed), and the irony is the useful part: the person who states a freeze rule is not thereby immune to it.

**3. THE STALE-DATA ASYMMETRY, generalized three times.** From a stale close, from cached order state, and from a run-level stamp: **you may raise a MISMATCH ALARM but you may never assert a MATCH.** Alarm permission is bounded to staleness that is *characterisable* (date proven, not stamp-inferred) and *self-limiting* (the clock's fault, ending at the next run) — without both bounds the alarm becomes the drumbeat class it was built to avoid. This rule now carries more Phase-21 weight than any other thing I wrote.

**4. THE THREE-STATE LATCH ACTION, sharpened by the operator against his own interest.** He asked that a decline capture its *reason*, and that no-action NOT be allowed to default to "away" without post-facto verification. That is the instrument refusing to flatter its subject, requested by the subject. The classification ladder (accept / decline+reason / away-attested / discipline-lapse / PRE_TELEMETRY), the exclusion of `pending_live` from every rate, and the objective-vs-attested away rate all descend from it.

**5. THE H1 CRITERIA AMENDMENT — governance, executed in the only clean window.** The frozen criterion required a Wilson-LB > 30% at n=20, which is 11 wins of 20 — a 55% raw win rate no measured cohort here approaches, and which would likely have REJECTED a genuinely profitable trend system. The replacement guard (no single trade ≥50% of gross profit) was chosen by stress test, not intuition: my own first proposal, leave-one-out, was tested and rejected because it fails a real fat-tailed trend shape. The operator argued the metric case independently and correctly — mean-R prices both legs and cannot be gamed by exit policy; win rate can move opposite to edge. He leaned toward a pre-registered note; I argued for a formal amendment; he changed course. The whole thing is only defensible because **H1 stands at 2/20 with no signal** — the window is open *because* nobody knows the answer, and it closes as the data arrives.

**6. T4 SOFTENED, AND I AM RECORDING IT AS A CAUTION AGAINST MY OWN LABEL.** The broad-watch closed mean moved −0.648 (n≈97) → −0.202 (n=312). Direction holds and nothing flips, but the margin thinned by two-thirds and the non-ETF slice is near break-even. The "FINAL" label attached on 07-05 meant *survived the engine fix*, not *cohort closed* — and it oversold stability to anyone reading the word rather than the caveat. Read #2 must RE-STATE the verdict at the larger N.

**7. THE PREMISE-SOURCING RULE** (`harness-architecture.md` §2) and its fifth row, PRESERVE THE QUOTE. Both were written because this generation kept meeting the same failure from different directions: a fact taken from a doc instead of code, a position taken from a stale message instead of the holder, a director's quoted words softened by a downstream editor into something that still *reads* like a quote. The last one is the dangerous one, because the failure is invisible from inside the loop.

**What I got wrong, since a successor learns more from this than from the rulings.** I invented a "~20 session" latch horizon, and it became its own citation — the plan back-solved 20 sessions from a date I had produced from nothing. Traced to `observe_max_pending_window_sessions = 30` and bound at the config source; FTRE's horizon moved to 08-31. I asserted view-telemetry was "essentially free" when writing on a GET is a seam the web layer lacks. I wrote an FTRE acceptance test that could not discriminate, because the table it queried had been created after the fire. I overstated an archive-lag clause and tightened it myself. I recommended a column before the migration that creates it had landed. Every one of these was caught by someone checking the artifact rather than accepting my framing — **the mechanism is the transferable part, not the list.** I am reliable on semantics and unreliable on remembered constants; a successor should assume the same of itself.

**On the working relationship.** The operator overruled me once (he was right), was convinced by me once (on the amendment route), and repeatedly sharpened proposals against his own convenience. CHARC beat my design twice this week by pricing a neighbouring step I had not traced, and I reversed both times. Neither of those is a failure mode to be reduced — it is the harness working. QA the divergence, including your own rulings.

**Open, with deadlines, in `rd-state.md` §5:** the three research-side gotcha-#30 instances inside my own measurement chain (before the August read — that read consumes that chain); D27, the bar-derived-vs-clock-derived `data_asof_date` collision; monthly read #2 in the first trading week of August; and three merge-blocking QAs (H1 amendment, 21-G, 21-B) with the binding 21-G-before-21-B ordering.

Encoding verified as the shape I demanded — one `_derives_from` predicate (`:2557`) applied uniformly (`:2616-2618`), meaning stated at the site (`:2520-2527`), no case list. `test_h_r3m1_regression_default_radio_manual_edit_top_level_unchanged` REWRITTEN with the supersession + lineage name preserved. **Two shipped contracts now superseded by this ruling (859, then this) — both mine to supersede: an implementer must not, an orchestrator should not, a director ruling is the instrument that does.** **B ROUND 3 (fixed tree, 2026-08-12) — MAJOR A: FINDING REAL, REMEDY REJECTED, AND THE FAULT WAS MINE.** I wrote that an id asserts *"the persisted values derive from the candidate this id names"* — that was the TEST for establishing the link AT POST, and I stated it as an INVARIANT the row must hold forever. **CORRECTED SEMANTICS: a top-level `schwab_order_id` asserts a HISTORICAL FACT — at recording time the operator LINKED this row to that order. The id names WHICH order; the values describe HOW it executed. Drift between them is a DATA-QUALITY question, NEVER an identity question, and a later correction makes the row a MORE accurate record of the SAME order.** **The live query is what refuted the remedy** (11 envelope-bearing non-entry fills; 1 drifted: **fill 40**, FTRE's exit, current `('2026-08-04',18.4,10.0)` vs candidate `('2026-08-03',18.4,10.0)` — **and its id is CORRECT**, the operator corrected the date toward truth): **the read-path re-derivation would have DEMOTED fill 40 and re-offered its order — the exact double-record my own ruling forbids, caused by my own remedy, on the row this whole programme was built around.** So: **read path owes NO re-derivation; correction writers owe NO id-drop; pre-fix residual population QUERIED = NONE (zero ids naming no candidate), FORWARD-ONLY on evidence; scope reaches no reconciliation writer.** **The real hazard the finding exposes is INVERTED — that a future reader or B round sees the drift and "fixes" it into the re-derivation. Take a COMMENT naming fill 40 as the reason.** **MAJOR B — BANKING REVERSED on new information: a sub-1-share execution 500s the trade-detail page** (`int(0.9)`→0; the dataclass guard rejects `quantity <= 0`; none of `_build_candidate`'s three refusal reasons catches it) — reachable from input both `fills.quantity REAL` and the Schwab model permit. **Remedy is a FOURTH REFUSAL REASON (visible degradation, not a crash), NOT the dataclass int→float migration — which stays BANKED with the sub-1-share limitation named IN it.** The narrow MAJOR-3 fix is unaffected and still ships. **MERGE-BLOCKING for me: a 500 from permitted input.** **B ROUND 3 SCOPE RULING (2026-08-12): the `SchwabSchemaParityError` 500 is BANKED, not fixed in-arc.** `exit_auto_fill.py:699` catches `(SchwabAuthError, SchwabRateLimitError, SchwabApiError)`; the MRO is `SchwabSchemaParityError → _RedactedMessageError → RuntimeError`, so a deliberately-raised parity error (`trader.py:665`) escapes and 500s the page. **`git log -S` on that symbol in that file is EMPTY — the arc never touched it.** **Evidence I ran rather than delegating: 7,795 production calls, ZERO schema-parity failures EVER; 3 lifetime errors on `accounts.orders.list` (1,278 of 1,289 are `marketdata.pricehistory`).** **THE BOUNDARY IS INTRODUCED-vs-PRE-EXISTING AND SEVERITY DOES NOT OVERRIDE IT** — the sub-one-share crash was fixed because the arc BUILT that machinery; this is untouched pre-existing. **If identical operator experience sufficed to pull a pre-existing defect in, the boundary would have NO CONTENT — every pre-existing defect produces some operator experience, so the exception swallows the rule and every bounded arc ends where its reviewer's imagination did.** The answer to "pre-existing is the excuse this project distrusts" is to make the banked item REAL, not to abandon the boundary: **BANK ≠ FORGET.** The item carries file:line, the handler tuple, the MRO (found only BY MRO, not by reading the except clause), and the zero-in-7,795 incidence WITH its query. **Scoped to the CLASS — *which handlers catch that tuple, and does the parity error escape each?* — not to the one line;** the right fix is wider than the arc could have taken. **The sub-one-share ADVISORY WORDING is MINE:** an advisory telling the operator to record by hand while the form's `min="1"` forbids it instructs an IMPOSSIBLE ACTION — strengthened beyond the orchestrator's scoping: it must say what the operator CAN do. **BANKED FOR THE RECIPE (orchestrator's, and it is worth more than the error that produced it): a reviewer not told what has been ACCEPTED re-finds it every round forever.** Declaring accepted contracts WITH their reasons — and explicitly INVITING challenge rather than forbidding the subject — suppressed re-litigation while B still returned 3 majors (round-2/round-3 pair is the evidence). **MY CAUTION: the list is mostly MY rulings and I have been wrong twice this week, both times corrected by a B round reading something I declared settled — so each entry carries its REASON, never a bare conclusion, or the list suppresses the challenge that corrected me on MAJOR A.** **CONDITION ATTACHED on the banked client-editable-envelope item:** its eventual brief must ENUMERATE the dismissals resting on it and re-adjudicate each — **a banked item silently governing other findings' dismissals is a load-bearing artifact, not a queue entry** (currently one live dependant, the `candidate_index` finding; MAJOR 2 is moot, closed not cited). **OPERATOR RULED 2026-08-11: "Flag noise is fine" — the MAJOR-1 override DECLINED, the FULL provenance rule stands UNQUALIFIED; the labelled floor is MOOT (do not ship it as a halfway house). Both contract tests (859 + 1625) rewrite to the flag semantics; MAJOR 1 leaves the cited list for the anonymous population. Nothing operator-pending remains on this arc; my merge gate re-runs at the fix's return.**
- **THE MERGE GATE — RESOLVED 2026-08-12, and MY boundary was creating the contradiction.** B is a WHOLE-ARTIFACT reviewer (that IS the delta-blindness cure), so on a file carrying real debt it returns real findings indefinitely — **"merge when B returns clean" is UNREACHABLE**, and held literally it forces either never-merge or the arc absorbing the surrounding surface, **the unbounded-arc failure my own boundary exists to prevent.** **ADOPTED (orchestrator's proposal, my concession): B IS CLEAN FOR MERGE WHEN IT RETURNS NO UNBANKED CRITICAL/MAJOR THAT THIS ARC INTRODUCED; pre-existing findings are BANKED — file:line, mechanism, reachability evidence — never merely dismissed.** The banking obligation IS the answer to "pre-existing becomes forgotten"; a gate reading INTRODUCED without it would be strictly worse than the unreachable one. **BINDING (their caution against their own proposal, the load-bearing half): IF AN ARC CANNOT SHOW THE EVIDENCE, THE FINDING DEFAULTS TO INTRODUCED** — the cheap failure is fixing what you did not break, the expensive one is shipping what you did, so the burden sits on whoever claims PRE-EXISTING. **Standard: a `git log -S` on the changed symbol or a `git diff --stat` proving the region untouched, QUOTED in the adjudication — never "this looks pre-existing."** **MY CLARIFICATION: CONTESTED provenance = INTRODUCED — where the symbol predates the arc but the arc changed its MEANING or made it REACHABLE (the truncation-under-a-new-float-comparison and the sub-one-share crash both). PROVENANCE IS ABOUT THE DEFECT, NOT THE LINE.**
- **THE TOLERANCE — I WAS WRONG IN ROUND 3 AND ALMOST WRONG AGAIN CORRECTING IT (2026-08-12).** I endorsed *"the tolerance is fine; the justification is the defect."* **Both were defective.** Reproducing the orchestrator's numbers, `math.isclose(a,b,abs_tol=1e-9)` returns **True** — `rel_tol` DEFAULTS to 1e-9, which at magnitude 1e7 is a tolerance of 1e-2 — so I nearly filed a correction against a correct finding. **Then I read the CALL: `:1418` passes `rel_tol=0.0` EXPLICITLY.** Against the real signature: large case residual **1.862645e-9 → False → a genuine duplicate offered CLEAN** (the silent miss the provenance rule forbids, reintroduced by the fix that closed its exact-equality version); small case 5e-10 → True → **different quantities flagged as the same row**. IN SCOPE (`git log -S "abs_tol"` → this arc's `5203caa9`). **MY LESSON: a comment defending a value is a claim ABOUT that value — auditing the claim's HONESTY while taking the VALUE on faith is HALF AN AUDIT**, the same shape as the paraphrase failure: inspecting the description instead of the thing. **And: reproduce against the REAL CALL SIGNATURE, never a plausible reconstruction of it — the default argument I did not check would have inverted the ruling.** The hybrid closes the large-magnitude miss and correctly does NOT tighten the small case (a spurious flag is adjudicated; a silent miss is not) — **the fix must SAY so, or the surviving looseness reads as an oversight.**
- **MY FORWARD TEST WAS CONFOUNDED — 96/97/98 CLOSE ON OTHER GROUNDS (2026-08-12).** I ruled the next reconciliation run would DECIDE their disposition. **It could not have.** Item 5 merged / correction 38 witnessed **2026-08-11 ~05:40Z**, and the FTRE `entry_price_mismatch` chain stopped at **run 97 (08-07)** — FOUR DAYS BEFORE THE FIX EXISTED. Runs 97, 98 and 99 (08-11T03:44, ~2h pre-correction) all ran on UNFIXED code; **only run 100 (08-12) is genuinely post-correction. Post-fix evidence = ONE run, not four.** *(The orchestrator read the sequence as four confirmations and concluded the fix took; the evidence is right and the conclusion does not follow — and it was MY test's premise that failed, not their diligence: they re-derived every state from the live DB, which is the only reason the sequence was in front of me.)* **DISPOSITION, on the DIRECT fact I reached past: the rows describe a condition CORRECTION 38 DEMONSTRABLY FIXED** (`entry_date` now 07-31, three coupled rows moved atomically, verified live) — whether the mapper also stopped re-emitting is a DIFFERENT question from whether these rows still describe something true. **Close all three `journal_corrected`, each reason citing CORRECTION 38 as the fixing event, NEVER the forward test; 97/98 keep their own ambiguous-match text and must not inherit 95's zero-delta wording.** **THE UNEXPLAINED CESSATION IS THE WORSE FINDING AND IS BANKED AS MINE: a reconciliation check that stops emitting for an unknown reason is a candidate SILENT FAILURE OF THE CHECK ITSELF** — the instrument going quiet instead of alarming, the class I have ruled on all week. Two benign explanations exist (the 7-day `lookback_days` window aging the entry out; the 08-06 exit-date correction changing the match) and **NEITHER IS ESTABLISHED.** Until one is, we do not know whether the check works or has stopped looking at FTRE. **It must never be recorded as "the fix took."** **THE LESSON: I built a test on the premise that a signal would PERSIST until my fix removed it, and never checked that the signal's absence had only one cause. A silence test needs its confounds enumerated BEFORE it is relied on** — inference from absence is exactly what I forbid everywhere else.
- **DISC 99 (AMN `untracked_broker_position`, material, unresolved) — THE CHECK WAS RIGHT WHEN IT FIRED, and I nearly closed it as stale.** Trade 20's entry fill carries `auto_fill_audit_at = 2026-08-09T06:17Z`; disc 99 was raised by run 98 at **2026-08-08T03:38Z**. **The position genuinely was not in the journal when the check ran — it was recorded a day later.** Not a false positive, not a timing artifact. **Close `journal_corrected`, with a reason stating the finding was CORRECT WHEN RAISED** and the position was subsequently journaled as trade 20 (recorded 08-09, entry dated 08-07) and has since closed. `mark_unmatched` would be false. **A row closed without that sentence reads as a false positive and teaches the next reader to distrust the check.**
- **TOOLING, MINE (2026-08-12): I posted a fenced code block via inline `--body` and BASH ATE IT** — the delivered message carried an EMPTY fence where the run table was, i.e. the one load-bearing piece of a message about my own instrument being unsound was the piece that did not arrive. **The `--body-file`-always rule was banked by the orchestrator this week and I said I adopted it. Write the body to a file and post it in the SAME STEP — never inline, never reach back for a path.**
- **MEASUREMENT TRAP — `pre_trade_locked_at` IS NOT EVIDENCE (banked 2026-08-09).** It equals `entry_date + 'T16:00:00'` on **20/20 trades, zero exceptions** — a synthetic restatement of the column it appears to corroborate, not a lock timestamp. Same family as gotcha #30 (a stamp standing in for per-row provenance), except here the stamp is DERIVED from the very value it seems to independently confirm. **Any decision-to-execution LAG analysis built on it would measure exactly ZERO on every trade, by construction, forever — and would look like a clean finding.** Build nothing on it until it is fed a real lock time or retired.
